Across four open-source LRMs and diverse reasoning benchmarks, BeaconKV generally outperforms existing compression methods, achieving up to $5.8\times$ memory reduction while nearly preserving full cache accuracy and improving throughput by over $4.3\times$.

</details>


### [63] [Beyond Homoscedasticity: Decoupled Uncertainty Optimization for Deep Imbalanced Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04995)
*Juncheng Zhou,Jiaxi Lu,Weijing Zeng,Zhong Li,Hao Qi,Jingsong Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DUO框架解决深度不平衡回归中尾样本欠拟合问题，通过条件高斯分布建模不确定性并用解耦均值-方差优化将不确定性转化为动态增强信号，同时设计分布引导对比学习缓解特征松散和跨标签语义纠缠，在多个视觉和生物基准上取得最优few-shot性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度不平衡回归任务中尾样本通常价值更高但更难学习。现有方法大多基于均方误差学习确定性点映射，隐含假设所有样本不确定性一致，忽视了长尾数据中广泛存在的实例级异方差性。此外，异方差负对数似然存在梯度耦合问题，在DIR场景下削弱困难尾样本的学习信号，导致优化惯性和尾部欠拟合。

Method: 1. 将回归目标建模为条件高斯分布，显式刻画实例级预测不确定性；2. 通过解耦均值-方差优化消除梯度耦合问题，并将不确定性转化为尾样本的动态增强信号；3. 设计分布引导的对比学习机制，基于样本分布重叠自适应构建正负样本对，缓解特征松散和跨标签语义纠缠。

Result: 在IMDB-WIKI-DIR、AgeDB-DIR和AAV2-DIR等视觉和生物DIR基准上，DUO在few-shot bMAE和GM指标上取得最佳性能，在few-shot MAE上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 不确定性感知的策略结合解耦优化与分布引导对比学习，能有效提升长尾回归中稀缺尾样本的学习效果，为深度不平衡回归提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Deep Imbalanced Regression (DIR) is pervasive in continuous prediction tasks across diverse modalities, such as age estimation, depth prediction, and protein mutation activity prediction, where label-scarce tail samples often carry higher practical value. However, most existing methods still learn deterministic point mappings under mean squared error or its simple variants, implicitly assuming a uniform uncertainty level across all samples and thereby overlooking the instance-wise heteroscedasticity that is widespread in long-tailed data. We further point out that even heteroscedastic negative log-likelihood suffers from a gradient coupling issue, which, under DIR scenarios, weakens the learning signal of hard tail samples and leads to optimization inertia as well as tail underfitting. To address this, we propose DUO, an uncertainty-aware long-tailed regression framework. Specifically, the proposed method models the regression target as a conditional Gaussian distribution to explicitly characterize instance-level predictive uncertainty, and transforms uncertainty into a dynamic enhancement signal for tail samples through decoupled mean-variance optimization. Furthermore, we design a distribution-guided contrastive learning mechanism that adaptively constructs positive and negative pairs based on the overlap between sample distributions, thereby alleviating feature looseness and cross-label semantic entanglement. Across visual and biological DIR benchmarks, DUO achieves the best few-shot bMAE and GM on IMDB-WIKI-DIR, AgeDB-DIR, and AAV2-DIR while remaining competitive on few-shot MAE.

</details>


### [64] [Solution-space heterogeneity shapes federated learning dynamics across partial differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05012)
*Ping Luo,Jiahuan Wang,Ziqing Wen,Tao Sun,Dongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出solution-space PDE-Dirichlet协议，通过最优传输在解空间中对联邦PDE学习中的非IID数据分布进行可量化的定义，并揭示Dirichlet浓度与客户端数据异质性之间的精确反比关系及其对优化收敛的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦科学机器学习允许各机构在不集中本地物理数据的情况下训练神经代理模型，但现有的PDE联邦学习研究缺乏对非独立同分布（non-IID）数据的可迁移定义。已有协议仅针对特定方程规则划分坐标、系数、边界条件或几何，无法通用化描述异构性。

Method: 提出solution-space PDE-Dirichlet协议。该方法将连续的监督响应转换为可复用的解空间分箱（solution bins），并通过这些分箱的几何结构利用最优传输量化客户端之间的实现分离程度。同时推导了种群分配异构性与Dirichlet浓度之间的精确反比关系，并建立响应异质性引发梯度不一致、局部更新分散和参数发散的数学条件。

Result: 在七个受控和公开PDE任务、三个神经算子家族和五个随机种子上验证：较低的Dirichlet浓度一致增加实现解距离和优化异构性。最终误差的退化程度依赖具体任务——低粘度Burgers方程在最异构设置下的影响最大（达到4.157个百分点），而额外的通信或更平滑的动力学可以减少最终差距，尽管参数分离仍然存在。

Conclusion: 该工作将可复现的几何机制与任务依赖的泛化结果区分开来，为评估非IID联邦PDE学习提供了统一的基础框架，有助于区分数据异构性本身的机制效应与具体任务带来的泛化影响。

Abstract: Federated scientific machine learning enables institutions to train neural surrogates without centralizing local physical data, yet studies of partial differential equations (PDEs) lack a transferable definition of non-independent and identically distributed data. Existing protocols partition coordinates, coefficients, boundary conditions, or geometries according to equation-specific rules. Here, we introduce solution-space PDE-Dirichlet, a protocol that converts continuous supervised responses into reusable solution bins and quantifies the realized separation between clients through optimal transport over the geometry of these bins. We derive an exact inverse relation between population allocation heterogeneity and the Dirichlet concentration, and we establish conditions under which response heterogeneity induces gradient disagreement, local-update dispersion, and parameter divergence. Across seven controlled and public PDE tasks, three neural-operator families, and five random seeds, a lower concentration consistently increases the realized solution distance and optimization heterogeneity. The degradation in final error is task dependent: the largest effect occurs for low-viscosity Burgers, reaching 4.157 percentage points under the most heterogeneous setting, whereas additional communication or smoother dynamics can reduce the final gap despite persistent parameter separation. These results distinguish a reproducible geometric mechanism from task-dependent generalization outcomes and provide a common basis for evaluating non-IID federated PDE learning.

</details>


### [65] [Amortizing Scaling Law Construction Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05016)
*Abhash Kumar Jha,Diana Alexandra Onuţu,Neeratyoy Mallik,Swagatam Haldar,Sam Laing,Niccolò Ajroldi,Shiwei Liu,Joaquin Vanschoren,Aaron Klein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效构建缩放定律（scaling law）的框架，将数据收集问题形式化为贝叶斯优化问题，通过逐步扩大计算预算并利用代理模型生成虚拟评估来替代全量实验网格训练，可在节省10-100倍计算开销的情况下获得与全网格训练相近的缩放定律拟合效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缩放定律是大模型训练设计的关键指导工具，但传统上推导缩放定律需要对超参数、token预算和参数数量进行全面网格搜索，计算开销极其昂贵。然而，拟合缩放定律实际上只需要不同计算规模下的最优损失前沿（best-loss frontier），大部分训练配置都被丢弃了，这造成了巨大的计算浪费。因此，如何在有限计算预算下高效收集足够数据进行缩放定律拟合是一个亟待解决的问题。

Method: 本文提出一个高效的缩放定律构建框架，核心包含三个要点：(1) 将数据收集过程形式化为贝叶斯优化问题，智能地选择需要训练的实验配置；(2) 提出在有限计算预算下比较不同缩放定律拟合方法的评估指标；(3) 在采集过程中逐步扩大计算预算（与实践中按计算量排序评估配置的方式一致），并利用代理模型对未观测配置进行幻想式评估（surrogate-fantasized evaluations），从而恢复完整的实验网格信息。

Result: 实验结果表明，逐步扩大计算预算的采集策略能显著提升缩放定律的恢复效率；结合代理幻想评估可以有效重建更广的实验网格。整体方法在与全密集网格（full dense grid）拟合效果相近的前提下，实现了高达10-100倍的计算节省。

Conclusion: 通过将缩放定律数据收集视为贝叶斯优化问题，并采用渐进式计算预算扩展与代理幻想评估相结合的策略，可以在大幅降低计算成本的同时获得准确的缩放定律拟合，为大模型训练的规模化法则推导提供了更经济高效的路径。

Abstract: Scaling laws guide the design choices for training large foundation models, but deriving them involves training an exhaustive grid over hyperparameters, token budgets, and parameter counts, which is computationally expensive. Fitting a scaling law, however, only requires the best-loss frontier across compute scales, discarding most of the trained configurations. We propose a framework for efficient scaling law construction that formulates data collection as a Bayesian optimization problem, and introduce metrics for comparing scaling law fitting methods under constrained compute budgets. We find that progressively expanding the compute budget during acquisition, mirroring the compute-ordered evaluation of configurations in practice, substantially improves recovery efficiency. Augmenting the observed configurations with surrogate-fantasized evaluations then recovers the broader experimental grid, allowing accurate scaling law fitting without training every configuration. Together, these can closely match scaling law fits over a full dense grid at computational savings of up to $10\text{--}100\times$.

</details>


### [66] [Confounding-Valid Conformal Inference for Counterfactual KPIs in Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05073)
*Abdessamed Qchohi,Jessica Moysen Cortes,Matteo Zecchin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CV-CCI方法，通过GESPI原理结合丰富的观测数据与有限的随机化数据，在网络反事实推断中同时保证有效性（应对隐藏混杂）和高效率（产生更紧凑的预测集）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络运营商需要利用日志遥测数据进行反事实推断以回答"what-if"问题，但日志数据可能遗漏控制器使用的变量，导致隐藏混杂并使统计保证失效。虽然可以通过随机化遥测解决，但随机化会干扰正常运营，导致随机化数据稀缺，仅依赖其产生的预测集信息量不足。

Method: 提出Confounding-Valid Counterfactual Conformal Inference (CV-CCI)，基于General Synthetic-Powered Inference (GESPI)原理，将丰富的潜在混杂观测数据与有限的随机化数据相结合；利用观测数据提升效率，利用随机化数据在任意隐藏混杂下保持有限样本覆盖保证。

Result: 在两个代表性无线接入网(RAN)控制任务上的实验表明，CV-CCI在隐藏混杂下保持有效性，同时产生的预测集比最先进的混杂有效基线方法更高效。

Conclusion: CV-CCI成功解决了网络反事实推断中观测数据存在隐藏混杂而随机化数据稀缺的矛盾，实现了有效性与效率之间的平衡，为网络运营商提供了可靠的"what-if"问题解答工具。

Abstract: Conformal counterfactual inference enables network operators to use logged telemetry to reliably answer 'what-if' questions about network operation. These answers typically take the form of prediction sets that contain, with a user-defined probability, the key performance indicators (KPIs) that would have been observed under alternative control actions. A key challenge is that logged telemetry may omit variables used by the controller, resulting in hidden confounding and invalidating the statistical guarantees of counterfactual analysis. In principle, this issue can be addressed using randomized telemetry, collected by assigning control actions independently of the network state. However, because such randomization may disrupt normal operation, randomized telemetry is typically scarce, causing counterfactual analysis based solely on it to produce uninformative prediction sets. To address these challenges, we propose Confounding-Valid Counterfactual Conformal Inference (CV-CCI), which combines abundant, potentially confounded observational telemetry with limited randomized data through the General Synthetic-Powered Inference (GESPI) principle. CV-CCI leverages observational data to improve efficiency while using randomized data to retain finite-sample coverage guarantees under arbitrary hidden confounding. Experiments on two representative radio access network (RAN) control tasks show that CV-CCI remains valid under hidden confounding while producing more efficient prediction sets than state-of-the-art confounding-valid baselines.

</details>


### [67] [Deep Microcompression: Structured Pruning and Bit-packed Quantization for Microcontrollers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05081)
*Opegbemi Matthias Busoye,Tolulope Matthew Busoye,Eghonghon-aye Eigbe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Deep Microcompression (DMC)，一种面向裸机微控制器的硬件感知深度学习推理流水线，集成了结构化剪枝、量化感知训练和定长比特打包技术，在LeNet-5上实现了55.8倍的权重压缩比（准确率98.77%），并首次实现了标准CNN在仅2KB SRAM的ATmega328P上的部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源极度受限的裸机微控制器上部署深度学习推理具有重大挑战，尤其是像ATmega328P这类仅有2KB SRAM的设备此前被认为无法运行标准CNN推理，现有方案如TensorFlow Lite仍存在二进制体积过大等问题。

Method: DMC采用三种核心技术的协同集成：(1)结构化剪枝减少冗余权重；(2)量化感知训练降低数值精度；(3)定长比特打包实现高效存储。该流水线能够生成无依赖的C库，提供确定性延迟保证，具备硬件感知特性。

Result: 在LeNet-5上实现55.8倍权重压缩比且保持98.77%准确率；在RP2040（Cortex-M0+）上相比TensorFlow Lite将二进制体积减少3倍且准确率相当；首次成功将标准CNN部署到仅2KB SRAM的ATmega328P上。

Conclusion: DMC通过硬件感知的模型压缩流水线，显著突破了微控制器上深度学习的部署极限，不仅超越了现有框架的压缩性能，还开辟了在极端资源受限设备上运行CNN的可能性。

Abstract: This paper introduces Deep Microcompression (DMC), a hardware-aware pipeline for deep learning inference on bare-metal microcontrollers. DMC integrates structured pruning, quantization-aware training, and fixed-length bit-packing to achieve a 55.8$\times$ weight compression ratio on LeNet-5 (98.77\% accuracy), generating a dependency-free C library with deterministic latency. On the RP2040 (Cortex-M0+), DMC reduces binary size by 3$\times$ versus TensorFlow Lite while matching its accuracy. Critically, DMC enables the first documented deployment of a standard CNN on the ATmega328P, a device constrained to 2KB SRAM, previously considered infeasible for CNN inference.

</details>


### [68] [NEAT-POCKET: Pocket-Conditioned Autoregressive 3D Molecular Generation with a Neighborhood-Guided Set Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05097)
*Roxane Axel Jacob,Daniel Rose,Thierry Langer,Johannes Kirchmair*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NEAT-POCKET是NEAT模型的口袋条件扩展版本，用于3D分子生成，能够在蛋白质口袋环境中逐原子生成分子，保持原子排列不变性并显式建模氢原子，在CrossDocked和SPINDR数据集上实现了具有竞争力的结构基生成性能，且采样速度远快于现有基准方法，同时天然支持口袋条件下的片段补全任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用AI驱动的从头分子设计加速早期药物发现，通过在靶蛋白结合口袋内直接生成新型配体。现有方法在生成性能和速度上存在不足，需要一种更快速且灵活的框架用于基于结构的药物设计。

Method: 将自回归NEAT模型扩展为口袋条件版本NEAT-POCKET，在蛋白质口袋环境中逐原子生成分子，保持原子排列不变性并显式建模氢原子。在CrossDocked和SPINDR数据集上评估完整分子生成及口袋条件片段补全性能。

Result: 在CrossDocked和SPINDR数据集上，NEAT-POCKET实现了具有竞争力的结构基生成性能，采样速度大幅快于现有基线方法，并能够自然完成口袋条件下的片段补全任务。

Conclusion: NEAT-POCKET是一个快速、灵活且实用的基于结构的药物设计框架，为早期药物发现中的从头设计和先导化合物优化提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: AI-driven de novo molecular design offers a promising route to accelerate early-stage drug discovery by generating novel ligands directly within target protein binding pockets. We present NEAT-POCKET, a pocket-conditioned extension of the autoregressive NEAT model for 3D molecular generation. NEAT-POCKET generates molecules atom by atom in protein pocket environments while preserving atom permutation invariance and explicitly modeling hydrogen atoms. Benchmarks on the CrossDocked and SPINDR datasets show that NEAT-POCKET achieves competitive structure-based generation performance while sampling substantially faster than existing baselines. Beyond full-molecule generation, NEAT-POCKET naturally enables pocket-conditioned fragment completion, a task directly relevant to lead optimization and scaffold elaboration. These results position NEAT-POCKET as a fast, flexible, and practical framework for structure-based drug design.

</details>


### [69] [A Comparative Study of Counterfactual Explainers for Graph Neural Networks Enabling Multiple Types of Graph Edit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05113)
*Maria Myrto Villia,Filippos Gouidis,Theodore Patkos,Panos Trahanias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对六种最新的图结构数据反事实解释方法进行了全面的比较研究，在多种真实和合成数据集上覆盖二分类和多分类的图分类与节点分类任务，并采用多种定量和定性指标评估各方法的性能，以揭示各方法的优缺点并指导未来研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图结构数据的反事实解释旨在找到最小的、现实的图修改来改变模型预测，但现有方法在解释质量、大小、覆盖度之间存在权衡，缺乏通用且高效的方法，亟需系统比较以明确各方法的优劣。

Method: 对六种最先进（SOTA）的反事实解释模型进行系统比较，使用多样化的真实世界和合成数据集，覆盖二元和多类别的图分类与节点分类任务，通过多种定量和定性指标评估其性能。

Result: 揭示了各方法在不同场景下的优劣势，表明现有方法在解释大小、覆盖度和质量之间往往存在权衡，没有一种方法在所有情况下都能表现最佳。

Conclusion: 研究为图反事实解释领域提供了全面的基准评估，指出了各方法的适用场景与不足之处，为未来的方法设计与改进提供了方向性指导。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations for graph-structured data seek to determine minimal and realistic modifications required in an input graph to alter a model's prediction to a predefined output. Although counterfactual explainers that support modifying the graph by both adding and removing edges have recently emerged, there is still a lack of general and efficient methods, especially when considering the quality of the generated explanations. Moreover, the problem remains far from solved, as existing methods exhibit different strengths and weaknesses, often trading off between explanation size, coverage and quality. For this reason, it is important to identify where each method performs well and where it falls short, so as to guide future research in the field. Thus, our study compares six state-of-the-art (SOTA) models on a diverse set of real-world and synthetic datasets, covering both binary and multi-class graph and node classification tasks, and evaluates their performance using diverse quantitative and qualitative metrics.

</details>


### [70] [Single-Query Black-Box Calibration Auditing via Logit Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05125)
*Roman Plaud,Antoine Saillenfest,Matthieu Labeau,Thomas Bonald,Willem Waegeman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用logit_bias参数以单次查询精确评估概率阈值的方法，从而为二进制任务构建了一致估计的真实校准误差（TCE）估计器，实现了对黑盒LLM的有效审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为零样本分类器的安全部署依赖于校准评估，但商业API提供商越来越多地隐藏连续输出概率，使得标准校准指标无法计算。需要一个绕过此不透明性的高效审计框架。

Method: 证明任何暴露logit_bias参数的LLM API都可以通过数学操纵，使用每个样本仅一次查询来精确评估概率阈值。基于此机制，提出了一种新颖且可证明一致的真实校准误差估计器。

Result: 所提出的方法能够高效地在二进制分类任务中一致地估计真实校准误差，为审计黑盒基础模型提供了可行且高效的框架。

Conclusion: 该方法通过利用logit_bias参数，为不透明的商业LLM API提供了可靠且高效的校准审计途径，推动了黑盒基础模型的安全评估。

Abstract: Evaluating the calibration of Large Language Models (LLMs) is critical for their safe deployment as zero-shot classifiers. Yet, commercial API providers increasingly hide the continuous output probabilities required by standard calibration metrics. To bypass this opacity, we demonstrate that any LLM API exposing a logit\_bias parameter can be mathematically manipulated to evaluate exact probability thresholds using strictly one query per sample. Leveraging this mechanism, we introduce a novel and provably consistent estimator of the True Calibration Error for binary tasks. Our approach therefore provides an efficient framework for auditing black-box foundation models.

</details>


### [71] [Coarse-Graining Hidden Representations: Unsupervised Neuron Selection via Mapping Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05126)
*Margherita Mele,Andrea Castagna,Roberto Menichetti,Raffaello Potestio,Alessandro Ingrosso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于映射熵（ME）的无监督神经元选择方法，通过粗粒化隐藏层、保留使映射熵最小的神经元子集，实现不依赖标签和梯度的神经元筛选。在教师-学生网络、非线性高斯过程和翻译增强MNIST任务中，ME选择的子网络优于同等规模的随机子集，尤其是在强压缩场景下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过参数化神经网络包含远多于任务名义需求的隐藏单元，但哪些神经元是关键的、以及这种区分是否能在无标签和无梯度的条件下从表征本身中辨识出来，是一个尚未解决的问题。

Method: 将神经元选择建模为隐藏层的粗粒化问题，通过保留神经元子集来评估每次选择对应的映射熵（ME）。映射熵度量丢弃部分神经元所带来的判别力损失，选择使映射熵最小的子集作为最具信息量的方案。该准则完全无监督，仅依赖于隐藏激活统计量。

Result: 在教师-学生网络中，ME优化恢复了最小教师一致表征，并按隐藏层残差变异的比例保留额外单元；在非线性高斯过程任务中，ME选择出功能性类映射的组合，且偏好类别随训练变化；在翻译增强MNIST任务中，ME选择的子网络在同等规模下优于随机子集，尤其在强压缩时优势最明显。

Conclusion: 映射熵提供了一种无需标签和梯度的神经元可辨识性度量，ME选择的子网络将构型可区分性与预测性能联系在一起，表明可判别性更强的神经元子集具有更优的泛化/预测能力。

Abstract: Overparameterized neural networks carry far more hidden units than a task nominally requires, raising the question of which neurons are essential and whether that distinction is legible in the representation itself, without labels or gradients. We cast neuron selection as the problem of coarse-graining the hidden layer by retaining a subset of its neurons, and score each putative selection by the mapping entropy (ME). This quantity measures the loss of discriminatory power inherent in discarding part of the network neurons, and the selection that minimises the ME is taken as particularly informative. This criterion is fully unsupervised, in that it depends only on hidden-activation statistics. In teacher-student networks, ME optimisation recovers the minimal teacher-consistent representation and retains extra units in proportion to the hidden layer's residual variability; in a non-linear Gaussian process task, it selects coherent functional-class mappings whose preferred class shifts across training. On this task and on translation-augmented MNIST, ME-selected subnetworks outperform random subsets of equal size, most clearly under strong compression - linking configurational distinguishability to predictive performance.

</details>


### [72] [MomentQuant: an even more minimalist interval method with linear time complexity for time series classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05136)
*Johann Faouzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MomentQuant算法，通过对原有Quant算法进行两个方面的加速改进：一是优化原始算法的实现，二是利用Cornish-Fisher展开推导近似分位数替代精确分位数，从而省去时间序列排序步骤，降低计算复杂度。实验证明MomentQuant比原始Quant更快，仅以极小的预测性能下降为代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列分类中，Quant算法虽然兼具高准确率和快速性，但在实际应用中推理远比训练频繁，需要进一步加速算法以满足实时应用场景的需求。

Method: 提出两种加速方案：(1)对原始Quant算法进行更优化的代码实现；(2)提出MomentQuant算法，使用Cornish-Fisher展开从矩信息推导近似分位数替代精确分位数，消除排序步骤以降低计算复杂度。

Result: 实验表明，优化后的Quant实现比原始实现更快，而MomentQuant比优化后的Quant更快，预测性能仅有微小下降。

Conclusion: MomentQuant在保持较高预测精度的同时显著提升推理速度，尤其适合推理频繁的真实世界应用场景。

Abstract: Time series data is very common in many real-world applications and in numerous domains, with increasing interest for automated information extraction using machine learning. One of these subfields is time series classification, which consists in assigning a label to each new, unseen time series. Many algorithms have been developed over the past decades, with the trade-off between predictive performance and computational cost being consistently discussed. Quant, an interval-based algorithm extracting quantiles from recursive, fixed, dyadic intervals, was shown to achieve high accuracy, while being very fast. We propose two changes to make this algorithm even faster. The first one is a better optimized implementation of the exact same algorithm. The second one is to derive approximate quantiles, using the Cornish-Fisher expansion, instead of exact quantiles. This change removes the necessity to sort the time series, leading to a smaller computational complexity. We call this novel algorithm MomentQuant. We provide evidence that our implementation of Quant is faster than the original one, and that MomentQuant is even faster than our implementation of Quant, at the cost of a tiny decrease in predictive performance. These improvements are especially relevant for real-life applications, where inference is performed much more often than training.

</details>


### [73] [From 80x to 385x: A Best-Matching-Unit Search at the L2 Roof, Measured Against a Symmetrically Tuned Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05138)
*Andrew James Amos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对SOM算法(SparseBin)与基线算法(cuSPARSE)同时进行对称性优化调参，通过四个优化杠杆（tile尺寸、簇划分、神经元轴分块、向量化加载）将SparseBin每轮训练速度提升5.6-10.1倍，同时使cuSPARSE提速2-3倍，最终内核达到L2带宽77%的峰值利用率，属于终端性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出GPU实现之间的对比常不对称——一方被精细调优，另一方仅按默认方式运行，导致对比不公平。作者希望通过同时调优双方算法来获得更公正的性能评估。

Method: 针对SOM训练中占主导的最近匹配单元（BMU）搜索，通过四个杠杆进行调优：tile大小、tile成员聚类、神经元轴分块、向量化加载。同时，对基线算法cuSPARSE的每个对应杠杆也施加等价的优化手段。

Result: SparseBin在32x32到512x512的地图尺寸下，每epoch速度较之前发布配置提升5.6-10.1倍；相对其背后CUDA实现的优势从~80倍提升至~385倍；cuSPARSE经优化后也提速2-3倍。调优后的内核达到L2带宽屋顶77%的峰值，其余单元利用率为40-65%，剩余优化空间被约束在~1.3倍以内。

Conclusion: 该结果是一个终端结果而非中间节点——由于L2带宽利用率已接近峰值，所有未经测试的杠杆要么因该界限在构造上受限，要么经测量无效，表明性能优化已到达实际极限。

Abstract: Comparisons between GPU implementations are usually asymmetric: one side is tuned by its author, the other is run as found. I report a programme that tuned both a novel SOM algorithm (SparseBin) and the baseline algorithm it was being compared to (cuSPARSE). The best-matching-unit search that dominates self-organizing map training was tuned through four levers - tile size, tile-membership clustering, neuron-axis chunking and vectorised loads - reaching 5.6-10.1x per epoch over the previously published configuration at map sizes from 32x32 to 512x512, and lifting the margin over the CUDA implementation behind our earlier MEDLINE atlases from ~80x to ~385x. cuSPARSE, the implementation SparseBin is compared against, received every lever with an analogue on its side, and became 2-3x faster in the process. The tuned kernel pressed the L2 bandwidth roof at 77% of peak with every other unit at 40-65%, bounding any further lever at ~1.3x - a terminal result rather than a waypoint, and every untested lever was either capped by that bound by construction or measured null.

</details>


### [74] [Beyond Stationarity in Time Series: Discovering Causal Structures and Latent Regimes via Markov Blankets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05150)
*Lei Zan,Charles K. Assaad,Emilie Devijver,Eric Gaussier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了RCBNB-MB算法，一种针对非平稳时间序列的因果发现方法，通过迭代分割时间序列为潜在因果机制区间并在每个区间内发现因果图，结合马尔可夫毯增强鲁棒性，在模拟和真实IT监控数据上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列因果发现方法大多假设单一、时间一致的因果结构，但现实世界中的动态系统常常表现出机制转换（regime changes），打破了静态因果结构的假设。这限制了传统因果发现方法在真实非平稳系统中的应用效果。

Method: RCBNB-MB采用迭代策略：首先将时间序列分割为不同的因果机制区间（机制定义为在时间子集内保持稳定因果结构），然后在每个机制区间内发现因果图。算法利用马尔可夫毯而非直接父节点来增强对因果发现误差的鲁棒性，并保留预测信息。

Result: 在具有已知真实结构的模拟数据集和真实IT监控数据上的实验表明，RCBNB-MB在准确检测机制变化及其关联因果图方面系统性优于基线方法。算法还具备在合理假设下恢复机制转换和因果图的理论保证。

Conclusion: RCBNB-MB作为非平稳时间序列因果发现的稳健且通用的框架，能有效处理机制转换场景，为动态系统的因果分析提供了新思路和实践验证。

Abstract: This paper introduces Regime-aware Constraint-Based and Noise-Based causal discovery with Markov Blankets (RCBNB-MB), a novel causal discovery algorithm for time series that relaxes the common assumption of a single, time-consistent causal structure. Time series are typically observed at discrete time points and often exhibit regime changes that challenge the assumption of a static causal structure, a limitation in many real-world dynamic systems. To address this challenge, RCBNB-MB identifies latent causal regimes, defined as subsets of time points within which a stable causal structure holds. The algorithm follows an iterative strategy that segments the time series into regimes and discovers the causal graph within each regime. By leveraging the Markov blanket rather than direct parents, RCBNB-MB gains robustness to errors in causal discovery and preserves predictive information. We provide theoretical guarantees for RCBNB-MB's ability to recover both regime transitions and causal graphs under reasonable assumptions. Furthermore, we validate its effectiveness through extensive experiments on simulated datasets with known ground truth and real-world IT monitoring data, where taking into account regime shifts is critical. Empirical results show that RCBNB-MB systematically outperforms baseline approaches in accurately detecting regime changes and their associated causal graphs, positioning it as a robust and versatile framework for non-stationary time series analysis.

</details>


### [75] [Phase Transition Frequency as a Training Time Predictor of Test Accuracy in ResNets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05194)
*Arunan J*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过75个实验（覆盖4个基准数据集和3种ResNet架构）实证检验了微调过程中类别可分性跳变次数与最终测试准确率之间的相关性。在标准i.i.d.分类任务（CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100）上观察到强负相关（r约为-0.84至-0.87），但在分布偏移基准（TinyImageNet、CIFAR-10-C）上相关性显著减弱。偏相关分析和与六种替代训练曲线信号的比较进一步界定了该发现的适用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探索一种简单、廉价的训练过程信号——类别可分性跳变次数——能否作为神经网络最终测试性能的早期预测指标，从而在无需训练完全部epoch的情况下对模型质量进行预判和诊断。

Method: 在四种基准（CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、TinyImageNet、CIFAR-10-C）和三种架构（ResNet-18/50/101）上进行5-10个种子的共75组微调实验，数计训练过程中离散类别可分性跳变的次数，计算其与最终测试准确率的Pearson相关系数；并通过偏相关分析（控制架构深度）和与六种替代训练曲线信号的比较来验证该信号的稳健性与独特性。

Result: 标准i.i.d.基准上获得强负相关：CIFAR-10上r=-0.84（p<10^-8, n=30），CIFAR-100上r=-0.87（p<10^-5, n=15）；分布偏移下相关性衰减至r=-0.45（TinyImageNet）和r=-0.19（CIFAR-10-C）。偏相关分析显示CIFAR-100上控制深度后r_partial=-0.69（p=0.007）。与六种替代信号比较中，跳变次数在CIFAR-100上表现最强，但在两个分布偏移基准上被其他信号超越。

Conclusion: 该研究将"类别可分性跳变次数"定性为一种仅适用于分布内训练质量的探测信号，而非通用的性能预测器。其预测效能在分布偏移场景下失效，且相较于有效秩、Hessian尖锐度等文献中更强的方法仍存在未探索的空间。论文提供了可实现为训练日志记录的低成本检测流程。

Abstract: The number of discrete class-separability jumps observed during ResNet finetuning is examined empirically as a predictor of final test accuracy. Across 75 experiments spanning four benchmarks (CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet, and CIFAR-10-C) and three architectures (ResNet-18, ResNet-50, and ResNet-101), with five to ten seeds per configuration, a strong within-dataset negative correlation is obtained on standard i.i.d. classification benchmarks: \(r = -0.84\) on CIFAR-10 (\(p < 10^{-8}\), \(n = 30\)) and \(r = -0.87\) on CIFAR-100 (\(p < 10^{-5}\), \(n = 15\)). Under distributional stress, the relationship attenuates: TinyImageNet yields \(r = -0.45\), and the CIFAR-10-C corruption benchmark yields \(r = -0.19\). Two additional analyses discipline the empirical claim. A partial correlation controlling for architecture depth, treated as a linear covariate, shows that on CIFAR-100 the transition count retains statistically significant predictive power (\(r_{\mathrm{partial}} = -0.69\), \(p = 0.007\)); the corresponding result under the stricter categorical conditioning is not established at \(n = 15\). A comparison against six alternative training-curve signals shows that transition count achieved the strongest correlation among the evaluated signals on CIFAR-100 and one of the strongest on CIFAR-10, but is dominated by other signals on the two stressed benchmarks. The comparison is restricted to training-curve-level signals; comparisons against effective rank, Hessian sharpness, Fisher information, margin, and neural-collapse measures, which are the strongest competitors in the current literature, are not part of the present study and remain open. The observation is presented as an in-distribution training-quality probe among a family of candidate probes, and an inexpensive detection procedure suitable for logging alongside a standard training loop is provided.

</details>


### [76] [Dimension-Adaptive Batched Lipschitz Narrowing Without Knowing the Zooming Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05214)
*Yasong Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Count-Adaptive BLiN算法，消除了ACE序列对缩放维度d_z的依赖，在d_z未知时仍能达到最优的O(log log T)批复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始A-BLiN算法中ACE序列的构造依赖于缩放维度d_z，当d_z未知时该算法无法直接应用，需要消除这一依赖。

Method: 不再根据d_z或缩放常数C_z选择下一边的长度，而是根据先前淘汰后存活的立方体数量来动态调整下一边缘长度，从而构建Count-Adaptive BLiN算法。

Result: Count-Adaptive BLiN不需要d_z和C_z，仍能达到Õ_d(T^{(d_z+1)/(d_z+2)})的遗憾界，且批复杂度为O_d(log log T)。结合原文定理10的自适应网格下界，当d_z未知时最优批复杂度为Θ_d(log log T)。

Conclusion: 本文成功去除了A-BLiN对d_z的依赖，证明在缩放维度未知的情况下仍可维持最优的遗憾与批量复杂度。

Abstract: The Appropriately Combined Edge-length (ACE) sequence in A-BLiN depends on the zooming dimension $d_z$. This note removes that dependence. The next edge length is selected from the number of cubes that survive the preceding elimination. The resulting Count-Adaptive BLiN algorithm does not use $d_z$ or the zooming constant $C_z$, yet it attains $\widetilde{\mathcal O}_d(T^{(d_z+1)/(d_z+2)})$ regret with $\mathcal O_d(\log\log T)$ batches. Together with the adaptive-grid lower bound in Theorem 10 of the original paper, the optimal batch complexity remains $Θ_d(\log\log T)$ when $d_z$ is unknown.

</details>


### [77] [FedDRAW: Federated Dual Reputation Annealing Weighting for Heterogeneous Multi-Institutional Chest Radiograph Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05223)
*Maryam Moradpour,Anne-Christin Hauschild*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种联邦学习服务器端聚合方法FedDRAW，结合数据量先验与客户端-全局参数余弦相似度，通过两阶段退火调度来优化加权，在胸片数据集的12个客户端分区场景中达到最优平均排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦平均（FedAvg）按本地样本数比例分配聚合权重，导致数据量小但信息量高的医院影响力被永久低估，而大客户端即使信息量较少的也会主导全局模型，造成诊断模型存在偏差。

Method: FedDRAW在服务器端将数据量先验与客户端与全局参数的余弦相似度相结合，采用两种耦合的退火调度：内部退火将客户端声誉从数据量先验逐渐转为相似度；外部延迟退火作用于softmax逆温度，在早中期保持选择性加权，在收敛时放松为均匀权重。

Result: 在12个模拟客户端分区场景和两个胸片数据集（CheXpert和ChestMNIST）上，与七个联邦基线方法对比，FedDRAW在AUC和几何均值（GM）指标下均取得最高平均排名，Friedman检验和Nemenyi事后分析确认方法间差异具有统计学显著性。

Conclusion: 调度两种信号而非仅根据样本数量固定权重，有助于减少诊断模型中的偏见。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence models are promising for medical diagnosis, but they require large numbers of unbiased data, which in medicine are distributed across hospitals and cannot be centralized to protect patient privacy. Federated Learning (FL) addresses this, since hospitals train one shared diagnostic model while patient data remain local. Training proceeds in communication rounds, in which each hospital trains the shared model locally and returns it to the server for merging by weighted average. This aggregation weight determines whose institutional knowledge shapes the result. Federated averaging (FedAvg) sets it in proportion to local sample count, so a small but informative hospital is permanently assigned a small influence, andl argest clients could dominate the global model even when they are less informative. We propose Federated Dual Reputation Annealing Weighting (FedDRAW), a server-side aggregation method that combines a data-size prior with the cosine similarity between client and global parameters under two coupled annealing schedules. An inner schedule shifts client reputation from the size prior towards similarity. An outer, deferred annealing schedule on the softmax inverse temperature keeps the weighting selective in the early and middle rounds and relaxes it to uniformity at convergence. We evaluate FedDRAW on 12 simulated client-partition scenarios of two chest radiograph datasets (CheXpert and ChestMNIST), against seven federated baselines under identical local training settings. FedDRAW achieved the highest average rank among all eight methods under both AUC and the geometric mean (GM) of sensitivity and specificity, which a Friedman test with Nemenyi post-hoc analysis confirmed to be a statistically significant difference between the methods. Scheduling two signals, rather than fixing the weights by sample count alone, could enable less biased diagnostic models.

</details>


### [78] [Hessian-based molecular conformation augmentation for a scalable and efficient strategy of machine learning interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05233)
*Bumju Kwak,Jeonghee Jo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于Hessian矩阵的数据增强方案（各向同性高斯位移UniAug和简正模式加权位移ModeAug），通过简单泰勒展开实现无需修改训练目标或架构的即插即用增强，提升了机器学习原子间势能模型在非平衡与平衡数据集上的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MLIPs仅基于能量和力进行训练，未充分利用对振动分析和过渡态搜索等实际应用至关重要的Hessian信息。现有的显式将Hessian纳入训练目标的方法需要架构修改，并因高阶反向传播带来显著计算和内存开销。

Method: 提出两种基于Hessian导出的数据增强方案：1) UniAug（各向同性高斯位移）和2) ModeAug（简正模式加权位移）。两者均利用简单泰勒展开实现有效增强，不改变训练目标，也不扩展autograd计算图，可无缝集成到现有架构和训练流程中。

Result: 在非平衡和平衡数据集上的全面评估表明，所提方法能提升模型精度，并提供实用的、针对具体任务的选择指导。

Conclusion: Hessian信息可以通过数据增强方式低成本地注入MLIPs训练中，避免了现有方法架构修改和计算开销大的问题，为需要精确Hessian信息的实际应用提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: While machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) have successfully learned potential energy surfaces (PES) and atomic forces, many practical applications, such as vibrational analysis and transition state search, rely heavily on the PES Hessian. Yet, standard MLIPs tend to be trained on energy and forces alone, leaving Hessian information largely unexploited. Meanwhile, existing methods that explicitly incorporate the Hessian into training objectives require architectural modifications and introduce significant computational and memory overheads due to higher-order backpropagation. To address these limitations, we propose two Hessian-derived data augmentation schemes: isotropic Gaussian displacement (\textbf{UniAug}) and normal mode-weighted displacement (\textbf{ModeAug}). Both methods utilize simple Taylor expansions, achieving effective augmentation without altering training objectives or extending the autograd graph. This allows seamless, plug-and-play integration with existing architectures and training pipelines. Comprehensive evaluations across non-equilibrium and equilibrium datasets demonstrate that our approach enhances model accuracy while providing practical, task-specific guidelines.

</details>


### [79] [PRICE: A Systematic Study of LLM Adaptation Choices for Bitcoin Price Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05235)
*Maryam Fakhari,Mehran Safayani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRICE框架，系统性地研究了大语言模型（LLM）在短期比特币价格预测中的多种适配选择（微调、数值表示、提示工程、推理和解码）如何联合影响预测性能。基于4比特量化的LLaMA-3 8B模型，PRICE整合了LoRA参数高效微调、递归多步推理、整数舍入数值表示、CTF提示和零温度解码。消融实验表明每个组件均有助于提升预测精度，对比实验显示PRICE在验证集和测试集上均取得了最低预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加密货币市场具有极端波动性和非平稳动态特征，传统预测方法面临挑战。虽然LLM在时间序列预测中展现出潜力，但在金融场景下，多种适配选择（如微调、数值表示、提示方式等）的组合效应尚未被充分探索。现有研究通常只关注单一适配维度，缺乏对LLM在金融时间序列预测中多种适配策略联合影响的系统理解。

Method: PRICE方法基于4比特量化的LLaMA-3 8B模型，整合了五大组件：(1) LoRA参数高效微调，支持在有限硬件上高效训练；(2) 递归多步推理策略提升预测精度；(3) 整数舍入数值表示以减少误差；(4) Context-Task-Format（CTF）提示工程，优于Chain-of-Thought、隐式Chain-of-Thought及few-shot提示；(5) 精确零温度解码提升递归预测稳定性。通过消融实验逐一验证各组件贡献，并与八个Transformer基础模型和时间序列基础模型进行对比评估。

Result: 实验结果表明：(1) PRICE在验证集和测试集上均取得最低预测误差，且在不同评估周期内保持稳健性能；(2) 消融实验证实LoRA、递归推理、整数舍入、CTF提示和零温度解码各自均对预测精度和可靠性有贡献；(3) 尽管PRICE基于主要预训练于文本而非时间序列数据的模型，其性能可与专门的时间序列基础模型相媲美甚至更优。

Conclusion: 研究表明，适配选择（adaptation choices）对LLM在数值时间序列预测中的准确性和稳健性具有决定性作用。适当的组合适配策略可以使基于文本预训练的LLM在时间序列预测中达到甚至超越专门化的基础模型，该发现为LLM在金融时间序列预测领域的应用提供了重要的方法论指导。

Abstract: Cryptocurrency markets exhibit extreme volatility and non-stationary dynamics that challenge conventional forecasting methods. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown promise for time series forecasting, the combined effects of adaptation choices remain largely unexplored in financial settings. This study introduces PRICE, a structured approach for adapting LLMs to short-term Bitcoin price forecasting. Built on a 4-bit quantized LLaMA-3 8B model, PRICE investigates how fine-tuning, numerical representation, prompting, inference, and decoding jointly influence forecasting performance. PRICE integrates Parameter-efficient fine-tuning with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), Recursive multi-step inference, Integer-rounded numerical representation, Context-Task-Format (CTF) prompting, and Exact zero-temperature decoding. Ablation studies show that each component contributes to forecasting accuracy and reliability. LoRA enables efficient training on limited hardware, recursive inference improves accuracy, integer-rounded values reduce errors, CTF prompting outperforms Chain-of-Thought, Implicit Chain-of-Thought (iCoT), and few-shot prompting, and zero-temperature decoding improves stability during recursive forecasting. Comparative evaluation against eight transformer-based and time-series foundation models shows that PRICE achieves the lowest forecasting errors on both validation and test sets while maintaining robust performance across evaluation periods. Despite being based on a model primarily pretrained on text rather than time-series data, PRICE achieves competitive or superior performance relative to specialized foundation models. These findings demonstrate that adaptation choices critically determine the accuracy and robustness of LLMs for numerical time-series forecasting.

</details>


### [80] [GLASS: Graph-Language Alignment with Spherical Scoring for Transferable Graph-Level Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05253)
*Xudong Wang,Chris Ding,Tongxin Li,Jicong Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GLASS是一个用于图级异常检测的框架，通过在图与语言对齐的统一表征空间（单位超球面）上实现跨域迁移。它利用Graph Descriptor Prompt（GraphDP）作为文本桥梁，结合多尺度Matryoshka表征切片和多模态评分机制，在12个基准和3个元域上取得了最优的平均AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图级异常检测（GLAD）在跨域迁移方面存在挑战，现有方法缺乏统一的表征空间来桥接不同领域之间的结构性和语义性差异，难以实现零样本或少样本的跨域异常检测。

Method: GLASS通过三个关键组件实现：(1) 将结构感知的图编码器与指令感知的文本嵌入在单位超球面上进行图-语言对齐，使用多切片软余弦目标；(2) 将局部、全局和语义图属性序列化为紧凑的Graph Descriptor Prompt（GraphDP），形成跨域文本桥梁；(3) 在超球面上进行密度估计实现异常评分，提出Spherical Multi-Modal Scoring（SMS），在图像和文本嵌入空间中实例化von Mises-Fisher核密度估计器，对结构性和语义性异常信号进行融合。

Result: 在12个基准数据集和3个元域上的实验表明，GLASS相比现有的先进GLAD基线方法取得了最佳的平均AUROC和排名，并展示了有效的跨域迁移能力。支持零样本异常检测和仅需少量正常样本的少样本自适应。

Conclusion: GLASS通过图-语言对齐和超球面密度估计，建立了一个统一且可跨域迁移的图级异常检测框架。该方法的概率性评分框架将角度k近邻评分作为高浓度极限特例，提供了结构和语义异常信号的可解释融合方式，验证了利用语言作为中间桥梁实现跨域异常检测的可行性。

Abstract: We introduce GLASS, a framework for graph-level anomaly detection (GLAD) that achieves robust cross-domain transferability through graph-language alignment on the unit hypersphere. GLASS builds a unified representation space by aligning a structure-aware graph encoder with an instruction-aware text embedding via a multi-slice soft cosine objective. Our framework serializes local, global, and semantic graph properties into a compact Graph Descriptor Prompt (GraphDP), creating a text bridge that enables domain-agnostic anomaly scoring. By enforcing multi-scale consistency through Matryoshka representation slices, the model captures anomalous deviations at multiple levels of granularity. For scoring, we formulate anomaly detection as density estimation on the aligned hypersphere and introduce Spherical Multi-Modal Scoring (SMS), which instantiates von Mises-Fisher kernel density estimators in both graph and text embedding spaces. This probabilistic formulation recovers angular k-nearest-neighbor scoring as a high-concentration limiting case and provides a principled fusion of structural and semantic anomaly signals. The shared text embedding space further serves as a cross-domain bridge: by encoding a target domain's GraphDP without target-domain training data, GLASS performs zero-shot anomaly detection, and with only a handful of normal examples, few-shot adaptation via reference-set calibration. Across twelve benchmarks and three meta-domains, GLASS obtains the best average AUROC and rank compared with recent advanced GLAD baselines and enables effective cross-domain transfer.

</details>


### [81] [How to Speculate about Uncertainty in Agentic Coding? A Draft-Model Gate Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05274)
*Konstantin Grotov,Valentin Malykh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为"投机性不确定性"(Speculative Uncertainty)的方法，通过一个小型开放权重草稿模型对黑盒LLM智能体的输出轨迹进行评分，仅从输出token即可预测失败信号，无需访问logits、权重或反复采样，从而实现预执行拦截以降低错误率和成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程领域部署的LLM智能体失败代价高昂：它们会自信地给出错误行为，而糟糕的动作往往要在昂贵的执行和重试之后才能被发现。需要一个能在执行前预测失败的信号。

Method: 利用投机解码（Speculative Decoding）的逆向过程：用一个小型开放权重草稿模型在单个前向传播中为智能体已生成的轨迹打分。从这些投机性交叉似然中提取相位感知（phase-aware）特征，区分推理和动作片段，并针对可验证的目标进行校准，最终生成失败可能性分数。

Result: 在Qwen3-Coder-480B和Claude 3.5 Sonnet两个软件工程智能体上实现了预执行否决门策略，将执行错误率降低6-8个百分点，token成本降低14-19%，无需重训练即可迁移到分布外基准，并能跨智能体模型泛化。

Conclusion: 投机性不确定性提供了一种从黑盒智能体输出中直接恢复可操作失败信号的有效途径，为路由、人工干预或额外测试时计算等下游策略提供了即用型信号，显著改善了部署效率和可靠性。

Abstract: LLM agents deployed for software engineering fail expensively: they act confidently wrong, and bad actions are recognized only after costly execution and retry. We present Speculative Uncertainty (SU), a method that recovers a predictive failure signal for a black-box agent from its output tokens alone, with no access to logits, weights, activations, or repeated sampling. Inverting speculative decoding, a small open-weight draft model scores the agent's already-generated trajectory in a single forward pass. From these speculative cross-likelihoods we extract phase-aware features by separating the reasoning and action spans, and calibrate them against a verifiable objective. SU produces a failure-likelihood score that any downstream policy, such as routing, human intervention, or extra test-time compute, can consume directly. To show the signal is actionable, we instantiate one such policy, a pre-execution veto gate, on software engineering agents Qwen3-Coder-480B and closed-source Claude 3.5 Sonnet, cutting execution error rate by 6-8 percentage points and token cost by 14-19% in deployment, transferring to out-of-distribution benchmarks without retraining, and generalizing across agent models.

</details>


### [82] [Learning from VAE Errors to support ECG-based Differential Diagnosis of Myocardial Scar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05294)
*Shayan Sharifi,Riccardo Treu,Ilaria Gandin,Federico Garoia,Marco Merlo,Giulia Cisotto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估了β变分自编码器（VAE）生成的ECG表征能否区分心肌病患者的LGE+（钆延迟增强阳性/心肌瘢痕）与LGE-状态。在300名受试者的本地队列中，基础模型ECGx.AI达到AUC 0.686，而浅层β-VAE仅为0.577；但基于DTW重建误差的分类达到AUC 0.643，且12导联中10个导联的误差在两类间显著差异，提示其作为瘢痕相关ECG改变的潜在标志物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LGE心脏磁共振是心肌瘢痕的关键标志，但可及性有限，因此需要基于常规ECG的无创筛查方法。

Method: 在300名心肌病患者的本地队列中，比较ECGx.AI基础模型与在正常PTB-XL ECG上训练的浅层β-VAE提取的32维特征，使用下游分类器和动态时间规整（DTW）重建误差进行评估。

Result: ECGx.AI+随机森林达到AUC 0.686；β-VAE+梯度提升达到AUC 0.577（灵敏度0.775）；DTW重建误差在12导联中的10个上在LGE+与LGE-间有显著差异，结合逻辑回归达到AUC 0.643。

Conclusion: DTW重建误差可作为心肌瘢痕相关ECG改变的潜在生物标志物，对辅助分类有价值；但β-VAE表征的判别能力有限，基础模型表现更优。

Abstract: Late Gadolinium Enhancement (LGE) on cardiac magnetic resonance is a key marker of myocardial scar, but its limited accessibility motivates routine ECG-based screening. We evaluated whether $β$-variational autoencoder (VAE)-derived ECG representations can discriminate LGE+ from LGE- cardiomyopathic patients in a local cohort of 300 subjects. We compared 32-dimensional features from the foundation ECGx.AI model with those from a shallower $β$-VAE trained on normal PTB-XL ECGs, evaluating downstream classification and Dynamic Time Warping (DTW)-based reconstruction errors. ECGx.AI reached an area under ROC of 0.686 with Random Forest, while the proposed $β$-VAE reached 0.577 with sensitivity of 0.775 with Gradient Boosting. Notably, DTW-reconstruction errors significantly differed between classes in 10 out of 12 leads according to Mann-Whitney U test and help in classification, leading to an area under ROC of 0.643 with Logistic Regression, supporting their potential as markers of scar-related ECG alterations.

</details>


### [83] [How Does mHC Use Its Residual Streams? Selective Routing and Near-Identity Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05309)
*Pengxiang Zhao,Xing Li,Xianzhi Yu,Wei Guo,Zhenhua Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了DeepSeek-V4-Flash中四流残差路径（基于Hyper-Connections及其流形约束变体mHC）的实际使用情况，发现读写路由集中但在深度上变化、残差混合主要发生在浅层且作用有限、模型仅实现了一部分四流mHC提供的灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hyper-Connections/mHC将残差路径从单流扩展为n流，但训练后的模型如何利用这种扩展能力尚不清楚——包括块读取/写入的广度、残差路径对流的混合强度以及各流是否携带不同表征。

Method: 使用有效流数量、跨流残差权重和流间余弦相似度三个指标，分析DeepSeek-V4-Flash四流残差路径的特性，并通过目标性干预实验验证其功能意义，包括替换混合器、固定混合器及保留最大路由权重等操作。

Result: 典型的注意力或FFN位置约使用两条流，主导流随层变化，表征方向性不同。第22-42层残差路径基本独立传递各流。将晚期混合器替换为恒等映射仅使C4困惑度增加1.9%且保持任务平均分不变，而替换早期混合器导致困惑度增加41%。将早期混合器固定到诊断均值，困惑度仅增加0.2%、平均分下降0.25个百分点；保留每位置三个最大路由权重，困惑度最多增加2.7%。

Conclusion: DeepSeek-V4-Flash仅实现了四流mHC所提供的一部分灵活性：单个块很少需要全部四条流，且晚期残差混合带来的可测量收益甚微。

Abstract: Hyper-Connections and their manifold-constrained variant mHC widen a residual pathway from one stream to n, yet how trained models use this capacity remains unclear: how broadly blocks read and write, how strongly the residual pathway mixes streams, and whether the streams carry distinct representations. We examine these properties in the four-stream residual pathway of DeepSeek-V4-Flash using effective stream counts, cross-stream residual weights, and inter-stream cosine similarity. Read/write routing is concentrated but varies across depth: a typical attention or FFN site effectively uses about two streams, while the dominant stream changes across layers and the representations remain directionally distinct. Residual mixing is modest and occurs primarily in early layers; in layers 22-42, the pathway mostly carries each stream forward separately. Targeted interventions establish the functional significance of these patterns. Replacing the late mixers by identity increases C4 perplexity by only 1.9% and preserves the six-task average score, whereas replacing the early mixers increases perplexity by 41%. Fixing each early mixer to its C4 diagnostic mean increases perplexity by only 0.2% and reduces the average score by 0.25 percentage points, showing that its site-specific structure matters more than its token-wise variation on the evaluated metrics. Likewise, retaining the three largest routing weights per token at every site increases perplexity by at most 2.7% and changes the average score by at most 0.4 points. Thus, the studied model realizes only part of the flexibility afforded by four-stream mHC: individual blocks rarely require all four streams, and late residual mixing provides little measured benefit.

</details>


### [84] [Optimal Rates for Agentic Networked Information Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05318)
*MohammadHossein Bateni,Zahra Hadizadeh,MohammadTaghi Hajiaghayi,Mahdi JafariRaviz,Shayan Taherijam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了网络化学习模型中信息聚合的误差率问题，将线性回归中M-覆盖路径的过采样误差从之前的O(M/√D)上界和Ω(M/D)下界之间的大间隙，精确收敛到：深度D<M²时误差为常数，D≥M²时误差为Θ(M²/D)。同时将这一最优速率推广到逻辑回归分类模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kearns等人（SODA'26）首次提出网络化学习模型，其中每个智能体只看到部分数据并仅传递自己的结论，这模拟了代理AI中信息聚合的核心模式。他们给出了上界O(M/√D)和下界Ω(M/D)，但两者之间存在显著差距。作者的目标是精确确定该问题的最优误差收敛速率。

Method: 作者首先对循环实例进行更精细的分析，将下界改进为D<M²时的Ω(√(M/D))。然后为每个深度D≥M²显式构造一个M-覆盖的深度为D的路径，使得过采样误差为Ω(M²/D)，同时该实例也给出了D<M²时的常数下界。此外还证明了对于任意固定分布，过采样误差沿路径几何收缩，排除了单个实例在所有深度上见证多项式下界的可能性。最后通过证明在这些例子中逻辑回归路径在再缩放下遵循最小二乘路径，将所有回归下界迁移到逻辑分类的logit传递模型。

Result: 作者彻底闭合了Kearns等人遗留的间隙：证明了M-覆盖深度为D的路径上过采样误差在D<M²时为常数，在D≥M²时为Θ(M²/D)。同时为逻辑回归分类（BCE损失）建立了相同的最优速率，上界O(M²/D)成立，且所有回归下界均通过再缩放转移。

Conclusion: 该研究为网络化学习模型中的信息聚合确立了精确的最优误差收敛速率，为理解和设计多智能体系统中的信息传播效率提供了理论基础，并在线性回归和逻辑回归两类重要模型上统一了结论。

Abstract: Building on the pioneering paper of Kearns, Roth, and Ryu (SODA'26), we study information aggregation in a networked learning model. The model captures a central pattern in agentic AI: each agent sees only part of the data and passes on only its own conclusion. Their model considers a linear regression problem with the mean squared error (MSE) loss. Agents sit in a DAG and each sees only a subset of the features and its parents' predictions, fits a linear predictor, and passes only its prediction forward. The benchmark is the full-feature learner that sees all raw features. A path of depth $D$ is $M$-covered if every block of $M$ consecutive agents collectively sees all raw features. Kearns, Roth, and Ryu proved that the excess mean squared error of the last agent on such a path is $O(M/\sqrt D)$, and gave a cyclic instance with excess error $Ω(M/D)$ for $D<M^2$.
  We close this gap: the correct rate is constant up to depth $M^2$, and $Θ(M^2/D)$ beyond it. We first give a sharper analysis of the cyclic instance and improve its lower bound to $Ω(\sqrt{M/D})$ for $D<M^2$. We then construct, for every depth $D\ge M^2$, an $M$-covered path of depth $D$ with excess error $Ω(M^2/D)$. The same instance gives the constant lower bound for all $D < M^2$. We also show that for any fixed distribution the excess error contracts geometrically along the path, ruling out any single instance that witnesses any polynomial lower bound at every depth.
  Finally, we prove the same optimal rate for logistic classification in the logit-passing model of Bateni et al., which considers the binary cross-entropy (BCE) loss. The same improved upper bound of $O(M^2/D)$ holds, and we transfer all the regression lower bounds by showing that on those examples the logistic path follows the least-squares path up to rescaling.

</details>


### [85] [Embedded Graph Flows for Categorical Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05328)
*Ethan Ma,Zihan Wang,Chris Siu Yeung Chow,Xinguo Feng,Qingqing Li,Rui Jiang,Naipeng Dong,Guangdong Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EGF是一种生成式模型，通过为节点和边类别学习连续嵌入，并用置换等变图Transformer将高斯噪声向这些学习到的端点传输，从而生成结构化分类图。在QM9和ZINC250k基准上优于DiGress和GruM等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分类图生成器通常将类别编码为固定的one-hot向量，这在类别间引入了人为的等距几何关系，限制了模型对类别之间真实语义关系的建模能力。此外，图的生成不应依赖节点顺序，需要一种同时处理类别连续嵌入和置换不变性的方法。

Method: EGF（Embedded Graph Flows）为节点和边类别学习连续嵌入，使用置换等变的图Transformer将高斯噪声传输到这些学习到的嵌入端点。模型在终端通过读出层将嵌入映射回离散图类别，从而完成图生成。

Result: 在QM9基准上，EGF在所有四个报告指标上均取得最佳结果，FCD达到0.150，远优于DiGress（0.717）和GruM（0.812）。在更大的ZINC250k数据集上，EGF在NSPDK核下取得最低的MMD，表明其与参考分子的局部子结构高度一致。

Conclusion: 通过学习类别嵌入替代固定one-hot编码，EGF能更有效地捕捉类别间的内在语义关系，在分子图生成任务上展现出竞争力的性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Generating categorical graphs requires choosing node and edge types that form a coherent structure without depending on node order. Many graph generators encode categories as fixed one-hot vectors, which can impose an artificial geometry in which categories are equidistant. We propose Embedded Graph Flows (EGF), a generative model that learns continuous embeddings for node and unordered-edge categories and transports Gaussian noise towards these learnt endpoints using a permutation-equivariant graph transformer. A terminal readout maps the embeddings back to discrete graph categories. Across molecular benchmarks, EGF achieved competitive performance. On QM9, EGF gives the best result on all four reported metrics among the three methods, including a Fréchet ChemNet Distance (FCD) of 0.150, compared with 0.717 for the categorical-diffusion baseline DiGress and 0.812 for the bridge-based baseline GruM. When applied to larger molecules in ZINC250k, EGF retains the lowest maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) using the neighbourhood subgraph pairwise distance kernel (NSPDK), indicating close agreement with the local substructures of the reference molecules. Our code is available at https://github.com/Trusted-System-Lab/EGF.

</details>


### [86] [Variational Continuation for Double Pendulum Periodic Orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05337)
*Leo Yao,Ziming Liu,Max Tegmark*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Hessian和自动微分的数值延拓方法，用于在动力系统中求解周期轨道。通过将周期轨道参数化为傅里叶级数，利用损失函数的平坦方向（零特征值方向）确定延拓方向，无需积分器和手推导数，能有效检测分岔并发现新的周期轨道类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统周期轨道延拓方法依赖手推的雅可比矩阵，过程繁琐且难以自动化；同时现有方法缺乏对轨道族分岔的系统性检测能力，需要更高效、更通用的数值延拓框架。

Method: 将周期轨道候选表示为傅里叶级数形式，定义基于物理微分方程偏差的损失函数；利用自动微分（机器学习中常用技术）替代手推雅可比矩阵；通过损失景观中零特征值的平坦方向确定延拓方向，使轨道搜索具有指导性且高效。

Result: 方法无需积分器，能精确初始化不稳定不动点附近的振荡；有效检测轨道族交叉和亚谐分岔。文中展示了双摆周期振荡从不动点出发的完整延拓，揭示了轨道族上的分岔并分类了周期轨道分支，同时发现了两个摆锤从不同时静止的周期轨道（此前文献中缺失）。

Conclusion: 该方法为周期轨道延拓提供了一种自动化、无积分器的替代方案，借助机器学习的自动微分技术降低了实现门槛，并能在实际系统中发现新的物理现象和有意义的周期轨道结构。

Abstract: We present a Hessian-based approach to numerically continue periodic orbits in dynamical systems. A loop (periodic orbit candidate) is parametrized as a Fourier series; a loss function is defined based on the deviation of the loop from the physical differential equations. Unlike previous work relying on hand-derived Jacobians, our method automates the process by leveraging automatic differentiation, a common machine learning technique. The continuation direction can be determined by the flat directions of the loss landscapes (directions with zero eigenvalues), making the search of periodic orbits efficient and guided. Our method is integrator-free, precisely initializes oscillations around unstable fixed points, and efficiently detects orbit family intersections and subharmonic bifurcations. As a demonstration, we present full continuations of periodic double pendulum oscillations from fixed points, showing bifurcations along orbit families and categorizing branches of periodic orbits. In particular, we find periodic orbits where both pendulum masses are never simultaneously at rest, which to our knowledge has been missing in the literature.

</details>


### [87] [Distill Globally, Adapt Locally: Reasoning Distillation and Product-Type Test-Time Training for Scalable Trade-Up Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05363)
*Siliang Liu,Mohammad Ghasemi,Sapan Patel,Amin Banitalebi-Dehkordi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两级框架，将大语言模型（LLM）的推理能力蒸馏到一个高效的嵌入对分类器中，用于"升级推荐"任务，在保持高效推理（仅需两个768维产品嵌入）的同时显著提升性能（AUC达0.941），相比直接LLM推理快约5000倍、成本低约10000倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM能够推理"升级推荐"中产品间的细微差别，但直接应用于数亿产品对在操作上不可行，需要一种既能利用LLM推理能力又能在工业规模上高效运行的方案。

Method: 两级框架：Level 1通过检索增强的少样本LLM教师生成结构化关系标签和自然语言推理理由，用于监督一个紧凑的嵌入对分类器（通过对齐和对比目标训练），推理时仅需两个预计算的768维产品嵌入；Level 2采用产品类型测试时训练（PT-TTT），利用少样本演示优化冻结学生模型上的轻量级类别适配器。

Result: 15.5M参数的四类推理蒸馏学生在8,352个人类标注基准上达到AUC 0.924（CI [0.918, 0.929]），优于仅标签学生的0.912；PT-TTT将AUC提升至0.941，平均精度从0.920提升至0.940；在100K对代理目录上，单台8-GPU机器上的推理比直接LLM推理快约5000倍、成本低约10000倍。

Conclusion: 该两级框架成功地将LLM的推理能力蒸馏为高效的非生成式学生模型，并通过产品类型特定的测试时训练进一步适配决策边界，在工业规模的升级推荐场景中实现了精度与效率的良好平衡。

Abstract: Trade-up recommendation identifies higher-quality alternatives that preserve a customer's purchase intent while offering upgraded benefits. Large language models (LLMs) can reason about such distinctions, but applying them directly to hundreds of millions of product pairs is operationally impractical. We introduce a two-level framework that distills LLM reasoning into an efficient non-generative student and adapts its decision boundary to product-type-specific trade-up criteria. At Level 1, a retrieval-augmented few-shot LLM teacher generates structured relation labels and natural-language rationales. These rationales supervise a compact embedding-pair classifier through alignment and contrastive objectives; at inference, the student uses only two precomputed 768-dimensional product embeddings, with no LLM calls or text generation. On a fixed human-annotated benchmark of 8,352 pairs, a 15.5M-parameter four-class reasoning-distilled student achieves AUC 0.924 (95% CI [0.918, 0.929]), compared with 0.912 for the four-class label-only student. At Level 2, product-type test-time training (PT-TTT) uses few-shot demonstrations to optimize lightweight category-specific adapters over the frozen student. PT-TTT improves AUC from 0.924 to 0.941 and average precision from 0.920 to 0.940. On a 100K-pair proxy catalog, the distilled student on a single eight-GPU machine is approximately 5,000x faster and 10,000x lower in estimated cost than direct LLM inference.

</details>


### [88] [RegionFed: Federated Learning for Personalized Query Understanding in Heterogeneous Retail Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05403)
*Quoc H. Nguyen,Ali Lafzi,Abhijeet Phatak,Siddharth Pratap Singh,Rohit Upadhyay,Yogananda Domlur Seetharama,Chittaranjan Tripathy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RegionFed是一个架构鲁棒的联邦学习框架，通过梯度级别的$\ell_2$冲突信号来诊断数据异构性、路由个性化策略并自适应控制个性化强度，在T5、RoBERTa、CNN等多种架构上无需代码改动即可部署，解决了参数级个性化方法在Transformer上崩溃的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售搜索系统服务不同地理区域，存在显著数据异构性，挑战隐私保护训练和模型个性化。标准联邦学习牺牲区域性能，现有参数级个性化方法在Transformer上因tied embeddings和LayerNorm交互而灾难性崩溃（T5上准确率低于10%）。

Method: RegionFed完全在梯度层面操作：利用区域梯度与全局梯度之间的$\ell_2$冲突作为统一信号，同时用于（i）诊断异构性程度，（ii）为每个区域路由到最便宜的充分个性化策略，（iii）自适应控制个性化强度。将模型视为可微黑盒，无需代码改动即可部署到不同架构。

Result: 在三个公开数据集（Amazon ESCI、Amazon Reviews、LEAF-FEMNIST）和四种架构上，RegionFed-Meta达到92.27%准确率，与隐私违规的集中式上界（92.04%）仅差0.23个百分点（在1σ内），同时提供$\epsilon\approx0.60$的差分隐私和$\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{T})$收敛保证。

Conclusion: RegionFed作为架构无关的梯度级联邦个性化框架，在Transformer上大幅超越参数级方法，在CNN上也保持一致改进，弥合了隐私保护联邦学习与集中式上界之间的性能差距。

Abstract: Retail search systems serve diverse geographic regions with distinct query patterns, vocabularies, and product preferences, creating significant data heterogeneity that challenges both privacy-preserving training and model personalization. Federated learning offers a natural solution for privacy, but standard FL methods produce global models that sacrifice regional performance, while existing personalized FL approaches operate at the parameter level and catastrophically collapse on modern transformers (below 10\% accuracy on T5) due to tied embeddings and LayerNorm interactions. We introduce RegionFed, an \textit{architecture-robust} federated learning framework that sidesteps this failure by operating entirely at the gradient level. RegionFed uses the $\ell_2$ conflict between regional and global gradients as a unified signal that (i) diagnoses heterogeneity, (ii) routes each region to the cheapest sufficient personalization strategy, and (iii) adaptively controls personalization strength. Because it treats models as differentiable black boxes, RegionFed deploys on T5-Small, T5-3B, RoBERTa, and CNN with zero code changes, providing large gains on transformers (where parameter-level methods collapse) and consistent improvements on CNNs. Across three public datasets (Amazon ESCI, Amazon Reviews, LEAF-FEMNIST) and four architectures, RegionFed-Meta achieves 92.27\%, closing the gap to the privacy-violating centralized upper bound (Centralized + Regional Weighting: 92.04\%, $Δ$=0.23pp, within 1$σ$) while providing $(ε{\approx}0.60)$-differential privacy and $\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{T})$ convergence.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [89] [EXAONE Forecast for Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04239)
*Seunghan Lee,Jaehoon Lee,Jun Seo,Tae Yoon Lim,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,Minjae Kim,Sungdong Yoo,Junhyeok Kang,Sangjun Han,Soonyoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EXAONE Finance，一个面向金融时间序列预测的基础模型。它采用无注意力架构（用因果1D卷积进行时间混合、用分组感知池化MLP进行变量混合）替代自注意力，并通过掩码上下文增强处理金融数据中普遍存在的缺失值问题。模型在涵盖股票、外汇、商品、加密资产、固定收益和宏观指标的大规模金融语料上预训练，在FinVerse基准的三个评估层级（点预测精度、横截面资产排序、组合盈利性）上均取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用时间序列基础模型存在三大局限：1）自注意力架构计算复杂度随序列长度和变量数量呈二次增长，难以应对金融场景中长序列、多通道数据；2）假设输入完全可观测，无法处理金融市场中普遍存在的数据缺失问题；3）预训练语料缺乏金融市场的独特动态特征。这些局限严重阻碍了通用TSFM在金融领域的应用。

Method: EXAONE Finance采用无注意力架构，用两个线性时间复杂度的算子替代自注意力：因果1D卷积负责时间维度混合，分组感知池化MLP负责变量维度混合。同时引入掩码上下文增强技术，在训练中向模型暴露连续缺失区间，提升对金融数据缺失的鲁棒性。模型在覆盖股票、外汇、商品、加密资产、固定收益和宏观指标的大规模金融语料上进行预训练。

Result: 在FinVerse金融预测基准上，EXAONE Finance在三个评估层级——点预测精度、横截面资产排序和投资组合盈利性——均排名第一，取得最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 通过采用高效的无注意力架构、针对金融数据缺失特性的训练策略以及涵盖多资产类别的金融专属预训练语料，EXAONE Finance有效克服了通用TSFM在金融预测中的局限，证明了金融领域专用基础模型的价值。

Abstract: This technical report presents EXAONE Forecast for Finance (EXAONE Finance), a financial time series (TS) foundation model (TSFM) tailored to financial forecasting. Recent TSFMs achieve strong zero-shot performance through large-scale pretraining. However, they are primarily developed for general-domain TS and largely rely on self-attention backbones whose computational cost grows quadratically with sequence length and variate count. Moreover, they assume fully observed inputs and are pretrained on corpora that fail to capture the unique dynamics of financial markets. These limitations hinder their applicability to finance, where long, many-channel, intermittently observed panels are common. To address these challenges, EXAONE Finance adopts an attention-free architecture, replacing self-attention with two simple yet effective linear-time operators: 1) a causal 1D convolution for temporal mixing and 2) a group-aware pooling multi-layer perceptron (MLP) for variate mixing. Furthermore, a masked context augmentation exposes the model to contiguous missing spans during training, improving robustness to the missingness pervasive in financial markets. EXAONE Finance is pretrained on a large-scale financial corpus covering not only equities but also foreign exchange, commodities, crypto-assets, fixed income, and macroeconomic indicators. On FinVerse, a financial forecasting benchmark covering diverse asset classes, EXAONE Finance attains state-of-the-art performance, ranking first across all three evaluation tiers---point-forecast accuracy, cross-sectional asset ranking, and portfolio profitability.

</details>


### [90] [From Matching Models to Recruiting Agents: A Systematized Narrative Review of AI Recruitment Systems, Evaluation, and Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04286)
*Ziyi Zhao,Guanzheng Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过系统化叙述性综述，梳理了人工智能招聘系统从双边检索和排序，到神经人岗匹配、大语言模型组件乃至工具型招聘代理的演进历程，分析了三个核心转变（从相似性到双向适配性、从模型到复合工作流、从离线预测到面向证据与生产力的评估），并识别了行为标签混淆、数据外效度受限、最终输出掩盖流水线故障等关键空白，提出了分阶段证据映射与评估议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 招聘领域的人工智能自动化对象已经从简单的档案对与排序列表，演变为检索证据、比较候选人和支持或执行动作的多阶段工作流。然而，现有综述缺乏对这一系统性演变的整合分析，特别是在评估证据层级、隐私考量与多方联合评估（效用、公平、隐私、安全）方面存在明显的研究空白，亟需系统化的梳理与议程提出。

Method: 采用系统化叙述性综述方法，利用目的性检索与编码协议（更新至2026年7月23日，并有截至2026年9月2日的定向补充更新），组织40篇代表性工作及配套的工业与法律来源，从三个耦合转变维度（相似性到双向适配性、模型到复合工作流、离线预测到证据与生产力对齐评估）进行分析，并区分文件级、配对级、列表级、案例级、轨迹级和结果级六种证据类型。

Result: 研究发现，在文档理解、检索、排序、评估、面试、寻源与人工交接等环节中，存在系统性空白：行为标签混淆了曝光度、偏好与资质；私有与合成数据限制了外部效度；最终输出分数掩盖了流水线中间失败；编码集内未直接评估隐私，且没有一行同时评估效用、公平性、隐私与安全性。基于此，提出了从评估证据到最强可辩护主张的分阶段映射，以及面向互相适配、证据基础、时间控制、选择性与可审计性的系统议程。

Conclusion: 招聘人工智能的进展标准应在于：工作流能否检索到正确的证据、保留不确定性、支持可争议的决策，并在明确的成本与风险约束下改善结果。该综述承认其描述范围限于所编码的文献集而非整个领域，并强调应以证据层级与可辩护主张的匹配作为评价此类系统的重要方法论准则。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence in recruitment has shifted the object being automated from profile pairs and ranked lists to multi-stage workflows that retrieve evidence, compare candidates, and support or execute actions. This systematized narrative review traces that development from bilateral retrieval and behavioral ranking through neural person--job matching, large language model (LLM) components, and tool-using recruiting agents. Using a purposive search and coding protocol updated through 23 July 2026, plus targeted updates through 2 September 2026, we organize 40 representative works with supporting industrial and legal sources. This synthesis is not a prevalence estimate. We analyze three coupled transitions: from similarity to reciprocal suitability, from a model to a compound workflow, and from offline prediction to evidence- and productivity-aligned evaluation. Across document understanding, retrieval, ranking, assessment, interviewing, sourcing, and human handoff, we distinguish field-, pair-, list-, case-, trajectory-, and outcome-level evidence. Persistent gaps arise because behavioral labels confound exposure, preference, and qualification; private and synthetic data limit external validity; final-output scores conceal pipeline failures; and, within the coded set, privacy is not directly evaluated and no row jointly evaluates utility, fairness, privacy, and security. These observations describe the coded set rather than the field as a whole. We therefore introduce a staged mapping from evaluation evidence to the strongest defensible claim, together with an agenda for reciprocal, evidence-grounded, temporally controlled, selective, and auditable systems. Progress should be judged by whether workflows retrieve the right evidence, preserve uncertainty, support contestable decisions, and improve outcomes under explicit cost and risk constraints.

</details>


### [91] [Harbor Adapters and Harbor-Index: Infrastructure and a Curated Meta-Dataset for Large-Scale Agentic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04298)
*Lin Shi,Haowei Lin,Zixuan Zhu,Xiaoyue Zhou,Xiang Li,Xiangning Lin,Yaxuan Deng,Han Xu,Yuangang Li,Shanda Li,Zizhao Chen,Hanwen Xing,Harsh Raj,Bo Chen,Quan Shi,Steven Dillmann,Yipeng Gao,Puneesh Khanna,Ruofan Lu,Chao Beyond Zhou,Michael Yang,Robert Zhang,Siyuan Chai,Jiayu Chang,Yizhao Chen,Xiaokun Chen,Yiwei Dai,Wenting Yang,Hange Liu,Minghao Liu,Zihan Wang,Adnan El Assadi,Benedikt Stroebl,E. Kelly Buchanan,Han Meng,Junwei He,Longxuan Yu,Radin Shayanfar,Yukyung Lee,Zhikang Dong,Allen G Hart,Anjiang Wei,Anurag Kashyap,Arpandeep Khatua,Audrey Jixin Zheng,Chengrui Ma,David Heineman,Dubing Chen,Hai-Anh Trinh,Haishuo Fang,Hefan Zhang,Hui Shen,Issa Sugiura,Jiankai Sun,Jiechao Gao,Junhong Lin,Junnan Li,Kai Yang,Lei Hsiung,Maoyu Wang,Mengze Tang,Nabil Omi,Negin Raoof,Nicholas Edwards,Octavia Guo,Orfeas Menis Mastromichalakis,Pengliang Ji,Przemysław Hejman,Qi Qi,Qunshu Lin,Richard Zhuang,Rui Yang,Ruichen Zheng,Ryan Marten,Shaghayegh Fazliani,Shizheng Hou,Sicong Jiang,Sijie Li,Song Bian,Terry Yue Zhuo,Tianqing Wu,Tom Tang,Wanjia Zhao,Weihao Xuan,Wenhua Liang,Xian Liu,Xin Lan,Xuan Zhang,Xuandong Zhao,Yanchuan Tang,Yifan Jiang,Yijiang Li,Yitong Guan,Yizhi Li,Yonghui Liu,Yuheng Tang,Yujun,Mao,Yunfei Zhao,Yuxin Wang,Yuxuan Tang,Zhenheng Tang,Zhifei Li,Ziruo Wang,Ziyu She,Kaiyuan Liu,Iheb Chaabane,Yuxin Tang,Xiangyi Li,Andy Konwinski,Boxuan Li,Leon Liangyu Chen,Alex Dimakis,Nicholas Carlini,Soroush Vosoughi,Di He,Etash Guha,Benjamin Feuer,Mike Merrill,Ludwig Schmidt,Alex Shaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Harbor Adapters，一个统一的智能体评测基础设施，包含80余个基准适配器、8个模型在54个基准上的大规模评测，以及由82个高质量任务组成的Harbor-Index精选集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在日益增长的智能体基准上评估智能体具有挑战性，因为这些基准通常需要复杂的环境和智能体集成，现有评测基础设施难以统一支持。

Method: （1）开发基准适配器将80余个基准移植到可评估任意智能体的统一框架，并通过代码审查和平行性实验验证；（2）对8个不同能力层级的模型在54个基准上进行大规模评测，每个模型分别使用Terminus-2和3种原生评测框架之一运行；（3）通过难度过滤、AI与人工审计及审计修复循环，从适配套件中提炼出Harbor-Index——涵盖29个基准的82个困难、多样且高质量任务。

Result: Harbor-Index在保持大规模智能体评测挑战性和广度的同时具备可负担的运行成本；没有任何模型-评测框架组合的通过率超过30%，最强的配置（GPT-5.5配合Codex）达到28.0%的通过率。

Conclusion: 适配器、评测结果、深入分析及Harbor-Index均以开源形式发布，以支持更可靠和全面的语言模型智能体评测。

Abstract: Evaluating agents on the growing number of agentic benchmarks is challenging because they often require complex environments and agent integrations. We introduce Harbor Adapters, a unified evaluation infrastructure for agentic benchmarks. Our work makes three contributions. First, we develop benchmark adapters that port more than 80 benchmarks to evaluate arbitrary agents, and validate them through rigorous code review and parity experiments. Second, we conduct a large-scale evaluation of 8 models spanning capability tiers across 54 benchmarks; every model is run with Terminus-2 and with one of 3 native harnesses. This enables a broader analysis of agent capabilities and failure modes than was previously possible. Third, we introduce Harbor-Index, a curated set of 82 difficult, diverse, and high-quality tasks spanning 29 benchmarks, refined from the adapted suite through difficulty filtering, AI and human audit, and an audit-and-fix loop. Harbor-Index preserves the challenge and breadth of large-scale agentic evaluations while being affordable to run; no evaluated model-harness configuration exceeds 30% pass rate, and the strongest (GPT-5.5 with Codex) reaches 28.0%. We release the adapters, evaluation results, in-depth analysis, and Harbor-Index as open-source artifacts to support more reliable and comprehensive evaluation of language-model agents.

</details>


### [92] [Data-Optimized Contingency Screening: A Machine Learning Approach to Power System Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04300)
*Joshua Salako,Folajimi Osikomaiya,Olakorede Olamiju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文利用机器学习算法（KNN、RF、SVM）对电力系统N-k故障场景进行安全等级分类（安全/中等/严重），使用Newton-Raphson潮流计算提取数据并以OPI为安全指标，结合SMOTE处理类别不平衡、PCA降维，在IEEE-14和IEEE-30总线系统上评估，RF表现最优（F1最高0.97），结果表明PCA对模型性能贡献大于SMOTE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力系统安全对稳定性和可靠性至关重要，有效分类故障（contingency）能够支持主动决策，防止大规模停电和故障扩散。

Method: 使用Newton-Raphson潮流法从N-k（k=1,2,3）故障场景中提取系统数据，以Overall Performance Index (OPI)作为安全指标；采用SMOTE解决类别不平衡、PCA进行降维；在IEEE-14和IEEE-30总线系统上训练KNN、RF和SVM三种模型，评估四种预处理配置（归一化、SMOTE平衡、PCA变换、SMOTE+PCA组合），以精确率、召回率和F1分数为评估指标。

Result: RF在IEEE-30和IEEE-14系统上取得最高F1分数分别为0.97和0.86；SVM在PCA下显著改善分类精度；KNN最适合SMOTE和PCA的组合转换；PCA对整体性能贡献大于SMOTE，SMOTE虽提升召回率但会引入误报。

Conclusion: 机器学习可作为传统故障分析的可扩展且高效的替代方案，提升电力系统实时安全评估能力。

Abstract: Ensuring the security of the power system is essential for stability and reliability, especially in the event of disruption. Effective classification of contingency in power systems enables proactive decision-making and mitigates large-scale breakdowns and failures. This study explores the use of machine learning algorithms to classify security levels of contingencies in power systems into safe, moderate or severe classes. For this approach, Newton-Raphson load flow method extracts system data from contingency scenarios, using Overall Performance Index (OPI) as safety measure. For data pre-processing, Synthetic Minority Over-Sampling Technique (SMOTE) and Principal Component Analysis (PCA) is used to address class imbalance and reduce dimensionality, respectively. K-Nearest Neighbours (KNN), Random Forest (RF) and Support Vector Machines (SVM) is trained and evaluated on datasets generated through N-k contingency scenarios for k equal 1, 2, and 3 on IEEE-14 and IEEE-30 bus systems using four hybrid pre-processing configurations: normalized, SMOTE-balanced, PCA-transformed, and a combined SMOTE PCA-transformed. Performance is assessed by precision, recall and F1 score, with priority given to the severe contingency classes. The RF achieved the highest F1 scores of 0.97 in IEEE-30 and 0.86 in IEEE-14, SVM benefits significantly from PCA and improves the accuracy of the classification, while KNN is best suited for SMOTE and PCA conversion. The findings show that PCA contributes more than SMOTE to the overall performance of the model. However, SMOTE improves recall but can introduce false positives and is therefore a compromise of accuracy. This study highlights machine learning as a scalable and powerful alternative to traditional contingency analysis, which improves the assessment of security in real time.

</details>


### [93] [Iris: Climbing to the Search Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04304)
*Ziyuan Liu,Hengqi Liu,Zichuan Wang,Yang Qin,Jiachen Liang,Xu Chu,Shaowei Chen,Yuantao Gu,Mu Chuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了两个开源搜索智能体Iris-mini（35B-A3B）和Iris-pro（397B-A17B），以及其背后的数据管线与训练流程。通过从网页超链接结构逆向构造多跳问答任务，并结合SFT-RL交替攀升的训练策略，两个模型在BrowseComp、BrowseComp-ZH、DeepSearchQA和HLE等基准上分别取得82.2/84.8/86.9/52.3和88.6/85.1/92.9/56.4的成绩，在各自参数范围内达到开源搜索智能体的最强结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索智能体是当前大语言模型的重要应用方向，但高质量训练数据稀缺且数据构造方法不透明。本文旨在解决搜索智能体训练中数据来源的真实性与质量问题，通过从真实网页超链接结构逆构任务来获得多跳搜索训练数据，同时探索大规模参数下搜索智能体的训练方法。

Method: 数据层：从网页超链接的实体图中逆构多跳链，将非答案实体改写为描述性引用以避免字符串匹配泄漏，并通过参考模型确保问题无法闭卷解答但需提供证据才能解决。训练层：将问题转为轨迹后进行两级过滤（轨迹级与回合级）再执行SFT；随后在实时搜索环境中进行RL优化，奖励判断器与观测摘要器部署在训练集群内，超长rollout在请求级中断后从已提交前缀恢复。提出SFT-RL攀升流程，将每一轮RL中难度最高且最有效的rollout回传给下一轮SFT。推理层：在固定工具集、上下文限制和判断器的前提下，评估时控制是否启用上下文管理功能，以对照其在基准上的贡献。

Result: 启用上下文管理后，Iris-mini在BrowseComp、BrowseComp-ZH、DeepSearchQA和HLE上分别达到82.2、84.8、86.9和52.3；Iris-pro分别达到88.6、85.1、92.9和56.4，均为各自参数范围内开源搜索智能体的最强结果。所有结果来自单一ReAct智能体，无子智能体与测试时验证。

Conclusion: 数据构造管线和SFT-RL攀升训练方法能有效提升搜索智能体的多跳推理与搜索能力，且推理时上下文管理带来的基准增益超过多数系统间报告差异，说明其在搜索场景中价值显著。作者计划开源模型权重及完整的数据构建、训练与评估方案。

Abstract: We present Iris-mini and Iris-pro, two search agents trained at the 35B-A3B and 397B-A17B scales, together with the data pipeline and training recipe behind them. Tasks are reverse-constructed from the hyperlink structure of a web corpus: we author multi-hop chains over an entity graph distilled from a seed page and its out-links, rewrite every non-answer entity into a descriptive reference so that no clue can be resolved by string matching, and admit only questions that a reference model fails closed-book yet solves once the supporting evidence is supplied. These questions are then turned into trajectories, which are filtered at both the trajectory and the turn level before SFT. The policy is then optimized by RL against live search, with the reward judge and the observation summarizer served inside the training cluster, and with over-long rollouts interrupted at the request level and resumed from their committed prefix at the next step. We alternate the two stages in a procedure we call SFT-RL climbing, returning the hardest solved and most efficient rollouts of each RL round to the next supervised pass. Because inference-time context management is worth more on these benchmarks than most reported differences between systems, we evaluate every benchmark both with and without it, holding the tool set, the context limit, and the judge fixed. All results come from a single ReAct agent, with no sub-agents and no test-time verification. With management enabled, on BrowseComp, BrowseComp-ZH, DeepSearchQA, and HLE the two models reach $82.2/84.8/86.9/52.3$ and $88.6/85.1/92.9/56.4$, the strongest overall results among open-source search agents in their respective parameter ranges. We plan to release the model weights together with the complete recipe for data construction, training, and evaluation.

</details>


### [94] [A Removal Based Approach to Improve LLM Faithfulness at Test-Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04343)
*Qinglan Luo,S M A Nahian,John Guttag,S. Mazdak Abulnaga,Katie Matton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种测试时方法来改善大语言模型解释的忠实性，特别是针对"不完整性"问题——即解释遗漏了影响答案的因素。方法是通过移除输入中未被解释提及的概念并重新查询模型，从而消除隐藏影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被广泛用于重要决策场景，其解释是审计模型行为的关键工具，但现有解释往往不忠实。已有方法主要针对"不健全性"（解释引用了未影响答案的因素），而忽视了"不完整性"（解释遗漏了影响答案的因素）这一维度。

Method: 提出一种测试时方法：从输入中移除那些未在模型解释中提及的概念，然后对缩减后的输入重新查询模型，从而消除未被提及因素的影响，同时保留被提及因素的影响。该方法无需修改模型参数，可应用于推理阶段。

Result: 在两个数据集、多个模型家族和两个独立的忠实性度量指标上，该方法相比标准提示和鼓励忠实性的提示，均改善了解释的忠实性。

Conclusion: 该方法具有模型无关性，可在推理时灵活应用，有效减少隐藏影响，提升LLM辅助决策的可靠性和安全性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for consequential decisions, making their explanations an important tool for auditing model behavior. Unfortunately, these explanations can be unfaithful, failing to reflect the actual reasoning underlying the model's decisions. We consider a setting in which an LLM provides both an answer and an explanation in response to a question. We identify two distinct dimensions of unfaithful explanations: incompleteness, meaning that the explanation omits factors that influence the answer, and unsoundness, meaning that the explanation cites factors that did not influence the model's answer. Existing approaches to improving LLM faithfulness include training-time methods, which require access to model weights and extensive computational resources, and test-time methods that largely focus on addressing unsoundness. We introduce a test-time approach that directly targets incompleteness. We remove from the input the concepts not credited in the model's explanation and re-query the model on the reduced input. This eliminates unmentioned influences while preserving the influence of mentioned concepts. Across two datasets, multiple model families, and two independent faithfulness metrics, our approach improves explanation faithfulness compared to both standard prompting and prompting to encourage faithfulness. Our method is model-agnostic and can be applied at inference time without modifying model parameters, providing a flexible mechanism for reducing hidden influences and improving the reliability and safety of LLM-assisted decision making.

</details>


### [95] [Why Better Models Can Create Riskier Systems: Evidence from LLM Agents in Financial Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04373)
*Jillian Ross,Eric So,Zoe De Simone,Charles Pozniak,Andrew W. Lo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这篇论文揭示了"能力悖论"：提升大语言模型的个体能力反而可能降低系统层面的表现。由于共享训练数据和架构，更强大的LLM倾向于表现出更高的行为相关性，形成无法通过多样化消除的系统性风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正被大规模部署在金融、内容审核、招聘等关键领域，但以往研究多关注个体模型能力的提升，而忽视了多模型协作系统层面的风险。作者假设共享训练和架构会导致更强大的LLM行为趋同，从而产生无法分散的相关性风险，这是一个被低估的系统性隐患。

Method: 作者构建了一个一般性框架来展示行为相关性如何产生不可分散的风险底线，并在金融市场中通过基于智能体的模拟进行验证。模拟中使用不同通用能力的LLM作为交易者，测试了从个体能力、行为相关性到系统风险之间的因果链条。

Result: 研究发现：（1）前沿LLM表现出显著相关的行为，且相关性随能力提升而增强；（2）当共享推理准确时，增加智能体参与可降低市场级风险；（3）当智能体共享错误信息环境时，相同的行为相关性反而成为风险的放大器。

Conclusion: 这些结果共同揭示了一个能力悖论：提升个体模型能力并不必然产生更好的系统级结果。该发现对LLM在关键领域的系统性部署提出了重要警示，其他领域是否出现类似动态仍有待实证检验。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are being deployed at scale in consequential real-world systems, from financial markets to content moderation to hiring. We show that improving individual model capability can degrade rather than improve system-level outcomes. We hypothesize that shared training and architectures can lead more capable LLMs to behave more similarly, creating correlated actions that do not diversify away. We develop a general framework showing how this correlation creates a non-diversifiable risk floor and test its predictions in financial markets using an agent-based simulation with LLM traders of varying general-purpose capability. We find that: (1) frontier LLMs exhibit significantly correlated behavior that increases with capability; (2) when their shared reasoning is accurate, increasing agent participation reduces market-level risk; and (3) when agents share a common misinformation environment, the same correlated behavior becomes a liability. Together, these results identify a capability paradox: improving individual models does not necessarily produce better system-level outcomes. Whether the same dynamics arise in other domains is an open empirical question.

</details>


### [96] [Corporate Language Model (CLM): Transforming Tacit and Fragmented Enterprise Knowledge into a Sovereign, Auditable, and Executable Corporate Intelligence Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04377)
*Fabricio C. Avini,Guilherme Trez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出企业语言模型（CLM）框架，通过五层能力平面和四大架构支柱，将企业隐性知识与显性知识转化为本体锚定的企业基础，实现可治理、可审计的企业级AI执行，并在巴西某JCI认证三级医院中验证了六个成熟度阶段中的三个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AI部署失败的根本原因不在于模型能力不足，而在于组织缺乏结构化基座来编码其决策、谈判和执行方式。通用大模型缺乏企业特定的本体先验知识，RAG方法脆弱且无法通向可执行行动，静态剧本虽有逻辑但无法推理或自适应，亟需一种将隐性知识捕获、本体锚定、自主部署和可审计执行作为整体协同设计的架构。

Method: CLM框架包含五层能力平面和四大架构支柱：(1)神经符号网格（Neurosymbolic Mesh）：将生成式模型与知识图谱耦合；(2)技能图（Skill Graph）：对可复用的策略、角色、异议和目标进行类型化与组合；(3)活体数字孪生（Living Digital Twins）：将功能领域建模为推理代理；(4)深度安全层（Deep Security Layer）：确保主权、可追踪性和人工监督。同时采用Spec-as-Code范式连接接地意图与可执行工件。

Result: 文章提出四项贡献：将CLM定义为一个独立的研究对象；引入技能图以实现可组合的、由构造上保证的可解释性；提出智慧倾听者效应（Wisdom Listener effect），即具备隐性知识能力的基础在使用中价值会复利增长，与动态能力和组织学习理论相连接；并在巴西一所JCI认证的三级医院提供了在LGPD法规下实施六个成熟度阶段中三个的实证证据。

Conclusion: CLM是"以基础为中心"这一架构类别的一个具体实例。企业AI的成功不仅依赖模型能力，更依赖将组织知识、治理机制和可执行推理作为整体进行系统化设计，CLM框架为此提供了完整且经过初步实证验证的架构方案。

Abstract: Enterprise AI deployments fail not from model inadequacy, but because organizations lack a structured substrate encoding how they decide, negotiate, and execute. Generic LLMs carry no firm-specific ontological priors; RAG remains brittle, with no path to executable action; static playbooks encode logic but cannot reason or adapt. This demands an architecture treating tacit-knowledge capture, ontological grounding, sovereign deployment, and auditable actuation as co-designed from the start. This paper introduces the Corporate Language Model (CLM), a framework transforming a firm's structured, unstructured, multimodal, and tacit knowledge into an ontology-grounded enterprise foundation upon which reasoning and governed execution are composed.
